## The open items of the state file

`awk '/^## Open items/{f=1;print;next} /^## /{f=0} f' .scratch/5-paper/orchestrator-state.md` prints the heading and its lead and no item:

```
## Open items (only what the user must rule on: a stop, and a proposal of the recurring-findings pass; repeated verbatim after the position line of the orchestrator's reports and the landing report until ruled or, under `self_rule: on`, until the orchestrator closes it as `plan-orchestration`'s `references/self-rule.md`, "Closing an open item", says)

A finding that is neither closed in the repair rounds nor fixed at landing is an open item here, and goes where `plan-orchestration`'s "What earns a step of its own" says; what is settled belongs in the closed list.
```

### The case the brief's rules got wrong, and the ruling

- Case 6: "`git grep -n -i "RAISE\|compliance_agent" -- skills/paper` prints nothing."
- The rule and the result: with `-i` the pattern matches the English word "raise" and Python's `raise`. On the unchanged tree it printed 106 lines, 62 of them in `templates/*.py` and `lookup.test.sh`, which the brief does not change, and one in the strategist page, which keeps that line. The copies of item 1 add more, such as `agents/revision_coach_agent.md` and `agents/peer_reviewer_agent.md` ("praise").
- The orchestrator's ruling (`.scratch/5-paper/agents/briefs/5.Q-cases.md`, round 0): "case 6 is `git grep -n "RAISE\|compliance_agent" -- skills/paper`, case-sensitive, and it prints nothing. On the unchanged tree it prints nothing and exits 1 (rerun by the session). It still catches academic-paper's `SKILL.md` line on `compliance_agent` and RAISE if that line is copied, which item 1 leaves out."
- The same ruling on the count of item 1: "that reading holds. The `## academic-paper` section has 41 `rebuild: paper` rows (rerun by the session). `SKILL.md` is one of them and is built by item 2. The strategist page is built by step 5.P. The other 39 are copied by item 1."

## The table of pages

Columns: the source file under `/Users/axelfaes/workspace/research-hub/.agents/skills/`, the page under `skills/paper/`, its line count (`wc -l`), the `diff -u` hunk count, and the edits by kind. The kinds are the edits of item 1: N, `paper`'s names for files, roles and records (`config.md` for the Paper Configuration Record, the run folder for the `phase<N>_*/` folders, `refs.bib` for `literature_corpus[]`, `\cite` for the `<!--ref:-->` marker, `/paper <mode>` for `academic-paper <mode>`, the role names); O, a sentence on a role, skill or mechanism `paper` has no counterpart for, left out (deep-research handoffs, `scripts/` verifiers, Schema 11, the Material Passport, the pipeline stages, `shared/` contracts); R, a reference to another academic-paper file named as the page that holds it; C, the character set and the no-history rule (`(v3.9.2)`, `#390`, `Kong #258`, `NEW`, the box and arrow characters, em dashes); Z, the Chinese-language rules left out; P, a rule that contradicts the manuscript prose standard left out; A, a sentence `paper` adds, named with its item.

The counts by kind come from replaying the editor commands on fresh copies of the sources in the scratchpad (`stage-0` to `stage-5`) and counting the hunks each command made, then reading every hunk of the last stage against the page (`hunklist.txt`, 498 hunks over the 50 pages). The columns give those per-command counts: char (the character-set command), names+refs (the role and reference commands), zh, join (the hard-wrap join), disc (the two history commands of the disclosure pages), hand (the hunks read by hand). The replay hunks overlap where one source hunk got several edits, so the columns add to more than the source-to-page hunk count.

## Judgment calls

1. Kept stops that academic-paper's text does not have: "Unknown mode", "An argument that does not exist", "The `.bib` is not one file" and "No prose standard" (`SKILL.md` 908 to 911). They refuse a call before any agent starts, the third follows from the LaTeX manuscript of (i) and the fourth from item 4. The reviewer may sort them otherwise.
2. The formatter's second start in format-convert, for the conversion patch, is a resumption of the same formatter, since academic-paper starts one formatter per run.
3. `commitments.md` in the run folder holds what academic-paper writes into Schema 11 and the Material Passport field.
4. The revision patch stop "You decide" of academic-paper's protocol is not drawn in `paper`'s revision figure, since every patch stop is in `STOPS_NOT_DRAWN` and the lead sentence of the figures names them.
5. The abstract-only protections that `paper` had taken from deep-research are left out with the deep-research sentences.
6. Case 5's one line is academic-paper's title of the revision coach page, "Reviewer Comment Parser and Revision Planner", copied word for word; it names no role.
7. The status lines (`sed -n 3p`):
   - 0020: "superseded by 0043 in its sentence on a run that continues an unfinished run, since `paper` no longer offers one"
   - 0027: "superseded by 0043 in its roles, their starts and the modes they run in, now academic-paper's twelve roles with the intake agent only in full, outline-only, abstract-only, lit-review and plan mode, the integrity and ethics checks belonging to `researcher` standing"
   - 0028: `Status: superseded by 0043`
   - 0029: "superseded by 0043 in the reading of the findings pass by a fresh peer reviewer and in the draft-review writer passes, now the draft writer's rewrite of each revision loop of full mode and revision's second writer pass, the incremental check of each later patch standing"
   - 0036: "superseded by 0043 in its starts, now academic-paper's, the starts of the literature search standing by 0042, in its draft-review stops, now academic-paper's round-2 decision and its stop after round 2, in its one stop for every claim the run cannot support, now academic-paper's stop at a claim not in its source, and in its deviations the anonymous copy and the palette outline, the caps on a fix, the pdfLaTeX engine and the ASCII entries of `lookup.py bibtex` standing"
   - 0038: "superseded by 0043 in its standing parts, the intake agent's split of the research question and the planner's reading of the parts, so that no part of it stands". Its line keeps its earlier parts rather than reading `superseded by 0043` alone, since 0042 and the strategist ruling superseded the other parts.
   - 0023, 0024, 0026, 0031, 0033 and 0034 are not changed: the change keeps their decisions (0024's claim going to the user with the URLs tried is academic-paper's stop now). 0017 and 0042 are not changed, as the brief says.
8. In `docs/academic-coverage.md` the strategist row says the Number of sources row, and the full-mode row no longer names one stop for every unsupported claim.

## Visible changes, before and after

- Modes: nine modes and two calls before; twelve modes after, with `revision-coach`, `rebuttal-audit` and `response-letter` new, and the two calls kept. The `rebuttal` routing (Use instead row, the refusal, Unknown mode) is gone.
- Roles: nine before (intake, literature strategist, planner, draft writer, visualization, peer reviewer, citation compliance, Socratic mentor, formatter); twelve after (planner replaced by structure architect and argument builder; abstract agent and revision coach added).
- Intake: before in every mode; after only in full, outline-only, abstract-only, lit-review and plan. The new optional row "Number of sources" in lit-review, full and outline-only, split into one target range per strategist start.
- Stops removed: an unfinished run, the items' approval, the plan's handoff, the draft review rejects the draft, a research question the materials do not answer. Stops added from academic-paper: a generator phase aborted, the parsed comments, the venue's policy, the uncertain AI use. "A claim the run cannot support" now stops at a claim not in its source.
- Full mode: the planner's outline and argument become Phases 2 and 3; the draft and the review run as the contract's four calls with at most two revision loops; the abstract is the abstract agent's patch.
- Revision: no intake agent, no items' approval, a round-2 re-review, and with referee comments the response letter `response-letter.md` beside the manuscript and `response-letter-check.md` with a verdict per point.
- Pages renamed or replaced: `planner.md` by `structure-architect.md` and `argument-builder.md`; `modes.md` by `mode-selection-guide.md`; `latex-template.md` by `latex-template-reference.md`; `patch-file.md` by `revision-patch-protocol.md`'s addition; `ai-disclosure.md` and `statements.md` by `disclosure-mode-protocol.md`, the policy pages and the funding and CRediT guides and templates.
- Figures: 12 mode figures under `docs/figures/paper/` (3 new), 25 comparison figures (the only-modes figure removed, revision-coach, rebuttal-audit and response-letter drawn on both sides).

## Anything in the brief that was wrong or impossible

1. Case 2 says the strategist diff shows "only the four Handoff lines of item 1 and item 6's lines". It also shows the pointer `references/intake.md`, "The evidence profile" changed to `references/domain-evidence-profiles.md`. The copied intake page has no section "The evidence profile", so item 1's rule that a reference names the page holding the file forces that line.
2. Item 4 says lit-review stays as 5.P left it. Its intake row names ("Materials in hand", "DOI checking", "Target formats", "The required elements") and its stop list name the old intake page, which item 1 replaces. They now read Existing Materials, Citation Verification, Output Format and Required Elements, and the unfinished-run stop is gone from its list. The search, the strategist and the identifier check are unchanged.
3. Case 6 as written could not hold (ruled above).
4. The negative-result rule of ruling AT is kept with its internal-only exception (`SKILL.md` Rules and Anti-pattern 22, `references/intake.md` row Internal only). The brief's item 3 does not name it; it stands as the plan's ruling.

Commands that wrote only to the scratchpad (the replay of the table of pages, the extraction of this list) are not listed.

3. Bash, "Apply the character-set edits to the copied pages"

````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q && SP=/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad; cut -f2 $SP/map.tsv | sed 's#^#skills/paper/#' > $SP/pages.txt; cat > $SP/charmap-cmd.txt <<'EOF'
python3 - $(cat pages.txt) <<'PY'
import re, sys
BOX = {'<U+2500>':'-','<U+2501>':'-','<U+2550>':'=','<U+2502>':'|','<U+251C>':'+','<U+2514>':'+','<U+250C>':'+','<U+2510>':'+','<U+2518>':'+','<U+2524>':'+','<U+252C>':'+','<U+2534>':'+','<U+253C>':'+'}
SIMPLE = {'<U+2260>':'!=','<U+2265>':'>=','<U+2264>':'<=','<U+00D7>':'x','<U+2208>':'in','<U+00B1>':'+/-','<U+2229>':'&','<U+21D2>':'=>','<U+25A1>':'[ ]','<U+2610>':'[ ]','<U+2713>':'[x]','<U+2026>':'...','<U+03B7>':'eta','<U+00B9>':'^1','<U+00B2>':'^2','<U+2074>':'^4','<U+2075>':'^5','<U+00B6>':'paragraph'}
def dash(line):
    line = re.sub(r'\|\s*<U+2014>\s*(?=\|)', '| - ', line)
    line = re.sub(r'(\d)<U+2013>(\d)', r'\1-\2', line)
    line = line.replace(' <U+2013> ', ' <U+2014> ').replace('<U+2013>', '-')
    if re.match(r'\s*(#+ |[-*] |\d+\. |\*\*)', line) and ' <U+2014> ' in line:
        head, tail = line.split(' <U+2014> ', 1)
        if len(head.split()) <= 12:
            line = head + ': ' + tail
    line = re.sub(r'\s*<U+2014>\s*', ', ', line)
    line = line.replace(', ,', ',').replace(',,', ',').replace(':,', ':').replace('(, ', '(')
    return line
for path in sys.argv[1:]:
    text = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    out, code = [], False
    for line in text.split('\n'):
        if line.lstrip().startswith('```'):
            code = not code
        for k, v in BOX.items():
            line = line.replace(k, v)
        line = re.sub('[<U+26A0><U+2605>]<U+FE0F>? ?', '', line).replace(' NEW', ' NEW') if ('<U+26A0>' in line or '<U+2605>' in line) else line
        line = line.replace('<U+00A7><U+00A7>', 'sections ').replace('<U+00A7> ', 'section ').replace('<U+00A7>', 'section ')
        for k, v in SIMPLE.items():
            line = line.replace(k, v)
        if code:
            line = line.replace('<U+2192>', '->').replace('<U+2190>', '<-').replace('<U+2193>', 'v').replace('<U+2194>', '<->')
        else:
            line = re.sub(r'\s*<U+2192>\s*', ' to ', line)
        line = dash(line)
        out.append(line)
    new = '\n'.join(out)
    open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(new)
    if open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() != new:
        sys.exit(path + ': read back differs')
PY
EOF
cd $SP && sed 's#\$(cat pages.txt)#$(sed "s|^|/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/|" pages.txt)#' charmap-cmd.txt > run.sh && sh run.sh && echo done; cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q && perl -CSD -ne 'while(/([^\x00-\x7E])/g){$c{$1}++} END{for(sort {$c{$b}<=>$c{$a}} keys %c){printf "U+%04X %d; ", ord($_), $c{$_}}}' $(cat $SP/pages.txt); echo
````

import re
def ed(path, pairs):

def ed(path, pairs):
    p = R + path
    t = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()

            raise SystemExit(f"{path}: {a[:80]!r} occurs {n} times")

    open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(t)
    if open(p, encoding='utf-8').read() != t:
        raise SystemExit(f"{path}: read back differs")
    print(f"{path}: {len(pairs)} edits")

import re
SP = '/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/'
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'

pat = re.compile(r'(?:\.\./)*(?:academic-paper/|academic-paper-reviewer/|academic-pipeline/)?(?:(?:references|agents|templates|examples)/)?(' + alt + r')(?![A-Za-z0-9_])')

12. Bash, "Rename the agent identifiers to paper's role names"

````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper && SP=/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad; cat > $SP/rolemap-cmd.txt <<'EOF'
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
SP = '/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/'
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'
ROLE = {'intake':'intake agent','literature_strategist':'literature strategist','structure_architect':'structure architect','argument_builder':'argument builder','draft_writer':'draft writer','citation_compliance':'citation compliance agent','abstract_bilingual':'abstract agent','peer_reviewer':'peer reviewer','formatter':'formatter','socratic_mentor':'Socratic mentor','visualization':'visualization agent','revision_coach':'revision coach','format':'formatter'}
alt = '|'.join(sorted(ROLE, key=len, reverse=True))
rows = [l.rstrip('\n').split('\t') for l in open(SP + 'map.tsv')]
def cap(s): return s[0].upper() + s[1:]
for s, p in rows:
    path = R + p
    t = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    o = t
    t = re.sub(r'^name: (' + alt + r')_agent$', lambda m: 'name: ' + p.split('/')[-1][:-3], t, flags=re.M)
    t = re.sub(r'\| `(' + alt + r')_agent`', lambda m: '| ' + cap(ROLE[m.group(1)]), t)
    t = re.sub(r'\[(' + alt + r')_agent\]', lambda m: '[' + ROLE[m.group(1)] + ']', t)
    t = re.sub(r'\[(' + alt + r')_agent \+ (' + alt + r')_agent\]', lambda m: '[' + ROLE[m.group(1)] + ' + ' + ROLE[m.group(2)] + ']', t)
    t = re.sub(r'(?<=^  - )(' + alt + r')_agent$', lambda m: ROLE[m.group(1)], t, flags=re.M)
    def prose(m):
        pre, role = m.group(1), ROLE[m.group(2)]
        if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $', pre):
            return pre + role
        start = pre == '' or re.search(r'(^|[.!?]\s|^\s*(?:[-*]|\d+\.)\s|\*\*|^#+ |\(|: )$', pre)
        return pre + ('The ' if start and not pre.endswith('(') and not pre.endswith(': ') else 'the ') + role
    t = re.sub(r'(^[^\n]*?|)`?\b(' + alt + r')_agent\b`?', lambda m: prose(m), t, flags=re.M) if False else t
    out = []
    for line in t.split('\n'):
        while True:
            m = re.search(r'`?\b(' + alt + r')_agent\b`?', line)
            if not m: break
            pre = line[:m.start()]
            role = ROLE[m.group(1)]
            if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $', pre):
                rep = role
            elif re.search(r'(^\s*$|[.!?]\s+$|^\s*(?:[-*>]|\d+\.)\s+(\*\*)?$|^\s*\*\*$|^#+ $)', pre):
                rep = 'The ' + role
            else:
                rep = 'the ' + role
            line = pre + rep + line[m.end():]
        out.append(line)
    t = '\n'.join(out)
    if t != o:
        open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(t)
        assert open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() == t
        print(p)
PY
EOF
sh $SP/rolemap-cmd.txt; grep -n -o -E ".{0,30}(the|The) (intake agent|literature strategist|structure architect|argument builder|draft writer|citation compliance agent|abstract agent|peer reviewer|formatter|Socratic mentor|visualization agent|revision coach).{0,15}" references/argument-builder.md references/failure-paths.md references/plan-mode-protocol.md | head -30

17. Bash, "Name the Paper Configuration Record as config.md"

import re, sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:

    n = re.sub(r'^(#+ .*)$', lambda m: m.group(1).replace('Paper Configuration Record', '\0'), t, flags=re.M)

    n = re.sub(r'\b(?:[Tt]he )?PCR\b', '`config.md`', n)
    n = n.replace('\0', 'Paper Configuration Record')
    if n != t:
        open(p, 'w').write(n); assert open(p).read() == n; print(p)

20. Bash, "Rename the agent identifiers to paper's role names"

````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper && SP=/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad; cat > $SP/rolemap-cmd.txt <<'EOF'
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
SP = '/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/'
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'
ROLE = {'intake':'intake agent','literature_strategist':'literature strategist','structure_architect':'structure architect','argument_builder':'argument builder','draft_writer':'draft writer','citation_compliance':'citation compliance agent','abstract_bilingual':'abstract agent','peer_reviewer':'peer reviewer','formatter':'formatter','socratic_mentor':'Socratic mentor','visualization':'visualization agent','revision_coach':'revision coach','format':'formatter'}
alt = '|'.join(sorted(ROLE, key=len, reverse=True))
rows = [l.rstrip('\n').split('\t') for l in open(SP + 'map.tsv')]
def cap(s): return s[0].upper() + s[1:]
for s, p in rows:
    path = R + p
    t = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    o = t
    t = re.sub(r'^name: (' + alt + r')_agent$', lambda m: 'name: ' + p.split('/')[-1][:-3], t, flags=re.M)
    t = re.sub(r'\| `(' + alt + r')_agent`', lambda m: '| ' + cap(ROLE[m.group(1)]), t)
    t = re.sub(r'\[(' + alt + r')_agent\]', lambda m: '[' + ROLE[m.group(1)] + ']', t)
    t = re.sub(r'\[(' + alt + r')_agent \+ (' + alt + r')_agent\]', lambda m: '[' + ROLE[m.group(1)] + ' + ' + ROLE[m.group(2)] + ']', t)
    t = re.sub(r'(?<=^  - )(' + alt + r')_agent$', lambda m: ROLE[m.group(1)], t, flags=re.M)
    def prose(m):
        pre, role = m.group(1), ROLE[m.group(2)]
        if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $', pre):
            return pre + role
        start = pre == '' or re.search(r'(^|[.!?]\s|^\s*(?:[-*]|\d+\.)\s|\*\*|^#+ |\(|: )$', pre)
        return pre + ('The ' if start and not pre.endswith('(') and not pre.endswith(': ') else 'the ') + role
    t = re.sub(r'(^[^\n]*?|)`?\b(' + alt + r')_agent\b`?', lambda m: prose(m), t, flags=re.M) if False else t
    out = []
    for line in t.split('\n'):
        while True:
            m = re.search(r'`?\b(' + alt + r')_agent\b`?', line)
            if not m: break
            pre = line[:m.start()]
            role = ROLE[m.group(1)]
            if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $', pre):
                rep = role
            elif re.search(r'(^\s*$|[.!?]\s+$|^\s*(?:[-*>]|\d+\.)\s+(\*\*)?$|^\s*\*\*$|^#+ $)', pre):
                rep = 'The ' + role
            else:
                rep = 'the ' + role
            line = pre + rep + line[m.end():]
        out.append(line)
    t = '\n'.join(out)
    if t != o:
        open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(t)
        assert open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() == t
        print(p)
PY
EOF
sh $SP/rolemap-cmd.txt; grep -n -o -E ".{0,30}(the|The) (intake agent|literature strategist|structure architect|argument builder|draft writer|citation compliance agent|abstract agent|peer reviewer|formatter|Socratic mentor|visualization agent|revision coach).{0,15}" references/argument-builder.md references/failure-paths.md references/plan-mode-protocol.md | head -30
````

exec(open('/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/ed.py').read())

def sect(start, end):
    a=old.index(start); b=old.index(end); return old[a:b].rstrip('\n')
def demote(s):
    out=[];fence=False
    for l in s.split('\n'):
        if l.startswith('```'): fence=not fence
        if not fence and l.startswith('#'): l='#'+l
        out.append(l)
    return '\n'.join(out)
filesec=sect('### The file','### Each section')
cites=sect('### Citations','### Results and the model they come from')

add = ("## Paper's addition: the LaTeX manuscript\n\n"
"This section is `paper`'s addition to academic-paper's agent. The manuscript is a LaTeX file and its `.bib`, and every change after the first draft is a patch, so the Output Format above is written as `main.tex` in the manuscript's folder, and the Draft Metadata and the word counts go into the agent's report. The agent reads the manuscript prose standard (the prose standard with the differences that `references/manuscript-prose-standard.md` of the `writing` skill folder lists), and the pages `references/rules.md` and `references/registers.md` of that folder, at the paths the session gives, and writes every sentence of the manuscript under them. It reads `references/latex-template-reference.md` for the preamble, the table widths, the `.bib` entry forms and the citation commands.\n\n"
+ demote(filesec)+'\n\n'+demote(cites)+'\n\n'+demote(markers)+'\n\n'+demote(prose)+'\n\n'+demote(neg)+'\n\n'+demote(fixes)+'\n')
add=add.replace('`references/latex-template.md`','`references/latex-template-reference.md`').replace('`references/patch-file.md`','`references/revision-patch-protocol.md`')
t=open(F).read().rstrip('\n')+'\n\n'+add
open(F,'w').write(t)
PY

old=open(SP+'old-formatter.md').read()
def sect(a,b):
    return old[old.index(a):old.index(b)].rstrip('\n')
parts=[sect('## The two patches','## Journal-specific formatting'), sect('### The change in the `.tex`','### What changes between styles').replace('### The change','## The change'), sect('## The conversion patch','## The format profile')]

out=[];fence=False

"This section is `paper`'s addition to academic-paper's agent. The formatter writes `format-report.md` in the run folder, in the form of the Output Package above.\n\n")
t=open(F).read().rstrip('\n')+'\n\n'+intro+body+'\n'
open(F,'w').write(t)

 ('## Output Format\n\n### Paper Configuration Record', '''### Step 14: Paper's Addition: the Rows of `paper`'s Checks

This step is `paper`'s addition to academic-paper's interview. It asks the rows that `paper`'s own checks read, each only in the modes listed against it, and writes each answer into `config.md`. |---|---|---|
| Number of sources | Optional. How many sources the literature search should end with. Left empty, the session decides the search as academic-paper's session does. | Internal only | Whether the user asks for the manuscript as an internal record, such as a record of every attempt, and not as work to publish. Default: no. The intake agent never sets it from the materials. | full, outline-only, abstract-only |
| Closing review | Whether the run ends with the `/writing` review of the manuscript, whose report a one-line reply answers. With no, the run ends after the compile, and the final message names `/writing` and `/paper revision <main.tex> <report>` for a later review. Default: yes. | full |

49. Bash, "Rename the bare role identifiers in the copies"

````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper && SP=/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad; python3 - $(sed 's#skills/paper/##' $SP/pages.txt | grep -v literature-strategist.md) <<'PY'
import re,sys
ROLE={'intake':'intake agent','literature_strategist':'literature strategist','structure_architect':'structure architect','argument_builder':'argument builder','draft_writer':'draft writer','citation_compliance':'citation compliance agent','abstract_bilingual':'abstract agent','peer_reviewer':'peer reviewer','socratic_mentor':'Socratic mentor','revision_coach':'revision coach'}
alt='|'.join(sorted([k for k in ROLE if k!='intake'],key=len,reverse=True))
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    t=open(p).read(); o=t
    out=[]
    for line in t.split('\n'):
        while True:
            m=re.search(r'`?\b('+alt+r')\b`?( agent\b)?',line)
            if not m: break
            pre=line[:m.start()]; role=ROLE[m.group(1)]
            if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $',pre): rep=role
            elif re.search(r'(^\s*$|[.!?]\s+$|^\s*(?:[-*>]|\d+\.)\s+(\*\*)?$|^\| $|^\s*\*\*$)',pre) or pre.endswith('| '): rep='The '+role
            else: rep='the '+role
            line=pre+rep+line[m.end():]
        out.append(line)
    t='\n'.join(out)
    if t!=o: open(p,'w').write(t); print(p)
PY

 ('it is a **required input to re-review and the Stage 4.5 integrity gate**, re-reviewers read it to see exactly which blocks changed (`ops_applied[]`, `fresh_block_ids`, `pure_move_pairs`) and which', 'it is a **required input to re-review and the incremental citation check**, re-reviewers read it to see exactly which blocks changed (the ops applied) and which'),
 ('## Mode B command sequence (one revision round)', '## Command sequence (one revision round)'),
 ('# 1. Anchorize / refresh the manifest (idempotent; safe on legacy drafts).\n#    Run at EVERY round entry, and rewrite nothing afterwards until apply, \n#    any rewrite (including a finalizer pass) invalidates the manifest.\npython scripts/ars_anchorize_draft.py draft.md',
  '# 1. Anchorize / refresh the manifest (the .tex is not changed).\n#    Run at EVERY round entry, and rewrite nothing afterwards until apply,\n#    any rewrite invalidates the manifest.\npython3 templates/anchorize_tex.py main.tex'),
 ('#    draft.md + draft.md.block-manifest.json + the round\'s Revision Roadmap.\n#    The writer emits phase6_*/revision_patch_round1.json (never a full draft).',
  '#    main.tex + main.tex.block-manifest.json + the round\'s Revision Roadmap.\n#    The writer writes patches/01-main.tex.patch.json (never a full draft).'),
 ('python scripts/ars_apply_revision_patch.py draft.md \\\n    phase6_revision/revision_patch_round1.json \\\n    --output draft.rev1.md',
  'python3 templates/apply_tex_patch.py main.tex \\\n    patches/01-main.tex.patch.json \\\n    --output main.rev1.tex'),
 ('# 4. Run your normal post-revision steps (finalizer / citation checks)\n#    on draft.rev1.md, then re-review with draft.rev1.md.apply-report.json\n#    attached.',
  '# 4. Run the post-revision steps (the compile and the citation check)\n#    on main.rev1.tex, then re-review with main.rev1.tex.apply-report.json\n#    attached.'),
 ('or `touched_ratio` strictly above **0.6** (the #424 ship decision; `insert_after`', 'or `block_touched_ratio` strictly above **0.6** (`insert_after`'),

 ('the flags stay recorded in the apply report either way. `--touched-ratio-threshold 1.0` disables the ratio trigger (the comparator is strict `>`); overriding 0.6 in pipeline runs requires a recorded user decision.', 'the flags stay recorded in the apply report either way.'),
])

90. Bash, "Restore the trace section in the VLM page and fix a dash artefact"

````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper && python3 - <<'PY'
exec(open('/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/ed.py').read())
sec='''## Figure/Table Trace

The VLM checklist above answers *"does the rendered figure match the source data?"*, a faithful-rendering check. It does **not** answer *"does the caption's interpretation follow from the data, and does the manuscript cite this artifact for a claim it actually supports?"* That is a different failure: a figure can render perfectly while its caption overstates what the data shows, or the manuscript can cite it for a claim the figure does not support. Kong et al. (2026) section 3.4 names this, "an AI-generated figure may look professional while containing ... invalid quantitative relationships", and it is the **visual analog of the prose partial-evidence trap**.

To make that checkable, the Figure Package carries a `figure_table_trace[]` block, one entry per visual artifact (figure **or** manuscript table) that links the rendered output back to its data and its claims. This is a **prose contract** written by the visualization agent; it is **not** a machine-validated schema and adds no lint, no JSON Schema, and no gold fixture.

### Trace block format

```yaml
figure_table_trace:
  - artifact_id: "fig-3"               # figure number or table id, stable within the package
    source_data:
      dataset_id: "abl-n128"           # logical dataset name
      file: "results/abl_n128.csv"     # path or pointer to the raw data
    transformation: {script: "scripts/plot_fig3.py", hash: "a1b2c3d"}
      # OR a precise manual-derivation pointer, e.g.
      # transformation: "manual derivation: see section 4.2 paragraph 2 (mean over 3 seeds, SE bars)"
    caption_claim: "Accuracy improves monotonically with N up to N=256."
    supported_manuscript_claims:                            # claims the figure is cited to support
      - claim: "Accuracy scales with model size up to N=256."   # claim TEXT (+ optional locator below)
        locator: "Results section 4.2, paragraph 3"              # where the manuscript makes it
    limitations:
      - "Only N=128, 256, 512 tested; the monotonic claim between those points is interpolation."
```

### Field rules

1. **`source_data`**: every claim-bearing artifact must point to a real dataset/file. A figure whose data origin is unstated is untraceable.
2. **`transformation`**: either a `{script, hash}` pair (reproducible) **or** a precise manual-derivation pointer naming the section/paragraph and the operation. A vague value (`"computed manually"`, `"see paper"`) is **not** sufficient and is untraceable.
3. **`caption_claim`**: the interpretive claim the caption makes. May be **compound** ("accuracy improves AND variance decreases").
4. **`supported_manuscript_claims`**: the manuscript claim(s) this artifact is cited to support, each as **claim text + an optional manuscript `locator`** (section/paragraph). It does **not** use a bare claim ID: the visualization agent can run before the draft and its `claim_intent_manifest` exist (figures are produced alongside the outline; the manifest is emitted by the prose agents during drafting), so a `(manifest_id, claim_id)` reference would dangle. When a manifest *does* exist (e.g. a revision-stage figure), an entry MAY additionally carry `manifest_id` + `claim_id` to join the scoped key, but the text + locator is the always-available primary. Each listed claim must actually reference the artifact in the manuscript and must not be overstated by it. Every listed claim must cite the artifact (forward), and every **substantive** manuscript use of the artifact must be covered by a listed claim (reverse): a one-sided trace that declares only the support the author wants seen, while the manuscript leans on the figure for an unlisted claim, is an omission. Incidental or structural mentions that assert nothing about the data ("see Figure N for the architecture", "results are summarized in Table N", a bare "(Figure N)" pointer) are exempt and must NOT be padded into the trace.
5. **`limitations`**: caveats the scholar knows about the artifact (e.g. "N=3 trials; error bars are SE not SD"). **The agent does not auto-detect missing limitations.** An empty `limitations: []` surfaces a named advisory (`[FIGURE-LIMITATIONS-EMPTY]`), not a silent pass; a **non-empty** limitation that never appears in the manuscript is a blocking issue (the agent knew it and the manuscript dropped it).

**Required keys.** All six keys, `artifact_id`, `source_data`, `transformation`, `caption_claim`, `supported_manuscript_claims`, and `limitations`, MUST be present on every entry. `limitations` is required but its value MAY be `[]` (so the empty-limitations advisory still fires); an entry that **omits** any key, including `artifact_id` (the key linking a figure to its trace) or the `limitations` key, is malformed for a claim-bearing artifact.

**Tables.** When a manuscript table has a `figure_table_trace[]` entry, the same rules apply.

---

## References
'''
ed('references/vlm-figure-verification.md', [
 ('---\n\n## References\n', sec),
 ('*arXiv:2604.05018*.: Section 4 Step 2', '*arXiv:2604.05018*. Section 4 Step 2'),
])
PY
LC_ALL=C grep -c '[^ -~]' references/vlm-figure-verification.md references/visualization.md; grep -rn '\.: \|\*\.: ' references | cut -c1-140 | head
````

91. Bash, "Count lines that continue a prose paragraph outside fences"

````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper && python3 - <<'PY'
import glob,re
tot=0
for p in sorted(glob.glob('references/*.md')):
    L=open(p).read().split('\n'); f=False; n=0; ex=[]
    for i in range(len(L)-1):
        a=L[i]
        if a.lstrip().startswith(('```','~~~')): f=not f; continue
        if f: continue
        b=L[i+1]
        if not a.strip() or not b.strip(): continue
        if a.lstrip().startswith(('|','#','>','<!--','---')) or b.lstrip().startswith(('|','#','>','-','*','+','```')) or re.match(r'\s*\d+[.)] ',b) or re.match(r'\s*[-*+] ',b) : continue
        if b.startswith('  ') and re.match(r'\s*([-*+]|\d+\.) ',a): pass
        n+=1; ex.append(i+1)
    if n: print(p,n,ex[:6]); tot+=n
print(tot)
PY
````

import re,sys

    for b in L:
        if b.lstrip().startswith(('```','~~~')):

                 and not b.lstrip().startswith(('|','#','>','```','<!--')) and not re.match(r'\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)]|- \[[ x]\]) ',b) \

            if ok:
                out[-1]=a.rstrip()+' '+b.strip(); n+=1; continue
        out.append(b)
    if n: open(p,'w').write('\n'.join(out)); print(p,n)

import sys
RH='/Users/axelfaes/workspace/research-hub/.agents/skills/'
m=[l.rstrip('\n').split('\t') for l in open(sys.argv[1]) if '\t' in l]
for src,dst in m:
    if not dst.endswith('.md'): continue
    try: S=open(RH+src).read().split('\n'); t=open(dst).read()
    except FileNotFoundError: continue
    pref=set()
    for l in S:
        s=l.strip()
        if s.startswith('**') and len(s)>=14: pref.add(s[:14])
    n=0
    for p in pref:
        k=t.find(' '+p)
        while k!=-1:
            ls=t.rfind('\n',0,k)+1
            if t[ls:k].strip() and not t[ls:].startswith('|'):
                t=t[:k]+'\n'+t[k+1:]; n+=1
            k=t.find(' '+p,k+1)
    if n: open(dst,'w').write(t); print(dst,n)

    for i,l in enumerate(L):

112. Bash, "Replace the paper mode graphs and run the figure script"

````
mkdir -p /private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/fig && cat > /private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/fig/simple.py <<'EOF'
SEARCH_STOP = "search rounds: end at any 3 of 5 conditions, or after 4 rounds"
PATCHED = "Script: each patch applied, the compile, the reference checks"
WRITING = "/writing review, unless you said no; the draft writer applies what you pick"


def _search(after: str) -> tuple[tuple[Node, ...], tuple[Edge, ...]]:
    """The session's choice of one literature strategist or several with a merge."""
    nodes = (
        Node("S", STRUCTURE, "choice"),
        Node("L", "Literature strategist, unless you give your own sources", "maybe"),
        Node("T2", STARTS, "maybe"),
        Node("M", MERGE + "review.md and refs.bib", "maybe"),
        Node("LR", "You review the sources, optional", "stop"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("S", "L", ONE),
        Edge("S", "T2", SEVERAL),
        Edge("L", "L", SEARCH_STOP, "back"),
        Edge("T2", "T2", SEARCH_STOP, "back"),
        Edge("T2", "M"),
        Edge("L", "LR"),
        Edge("M", "LR"),
        Edge("LR", after),
    )
    return nodes, edges


def paper_full_graph() -> Graph:
    search_nodes, search_edges = _search("A")
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: you confirm", "stop"),
        *search_nodes,
        Node("A", "Structure architect"),
        Node("OA", "Outline approval", "stop"),
        Node("B", "Argument builder"),
        Node("W", "Draft writer, 2 calls: main.tex"),
        Node("V", "Visualization, with data: the figures placed by patch", "maybe"),
        Node("C", "Citation compliance: each claim against its source's full text"),
        Node("AB", "Abstract agent: a patch"),
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer, 2 calls"),
        Node("WR", "Draft writer: the whole draft again, as one patch"),
        Node("IC", "Citation compliance: the cited sentences of the patch"),
        Node("SD", "You decide", "stop"),
        Node("SC", "A claim the run cannot support", "stop"),
        Node("CF", PATCHED, "script"),
        Node("F", "Formatter: targets, cover letter, AI disclosure, checklist"),
        Node("WG", WRITING, "maybe"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("I", "S"),
        *search_edges,
        Edge("A", "OA"),
        Edge("OA", "A", "restructuring, no cap", "back"),
        Edge("OA", "B"),
        Edge("B", "W"),
        Edge("B", "V"),
        Edge("V", "V", "checks the rendered figure, up to 2 fixes, optional", "back"),
        Edge("W", "C"),
        Edge("W", "AB"),
        Edge("C", "SC", "a claim not held, after every claim is judged"),
        Edge("C", "P"),
        Edge("AB", "P"),
        Edge("V", "P"),
        Edge("P", "WR", "a revision: round 2, once", "dotted"),
        Edge("WR", "IC"),
        Edge("IC", "P", "", "back"),
        Edge("P", "SD", "after round 2 a score under 6.5 with a Critical issue open"),
        Edge("P", "CF"),
        Edge("CF", "F"),
        Edge("F", "F", "a checklist item fails, no cap", "back"),
        Edge("F", "WG"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_revision_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer: the comments as a roadmap"),
        Node("W", "Draft writer: a patch per item"),
        Node("SD", "You decide", "stop"),
        Node("C", "Citation compliance: each claim against its source's full text"),
        Node("SC", "A claim the run cannot support", "stop"),
        Node("R2", "Peer reviewer: the re-review"),
        Node("W2", "Draft writer: the items not resolved", "maybe"),
        Node("CF", PATCHED, "script"),
        Node("RL", "Draft writer: the response letter, for referee comments", "maybe"),
        Node("RC", "Peer reviewer: the letter checked, a verdict per point", "maybe"),
        Node("WG", WRITING, "maybe"),
        Node("X", "Revised draft, the rest as limitations"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("P", "W"),
        Edge("W", "SD", "a structural patch, or a patch rejected twice"),
        Edge("W", "C"),
        Edge("C", "SC", "a claim not held"),
        Edge("C", "R2", "round 2, once"),
        Edge("R2", "W2"),
        Edge("W2", "CF"),
        Edge("R2", "CF"),
        Edge("CF", "RL"),
        Edge("RL", "RC"),
        Edge("RC", "WG"),
        Edge("CF", "WG", "a /writing report as the comments"),
        Edge("WG", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_outline_only_graph() -> Graph:
    search_nodes, search_edges = _search("A")
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: you confirm", "stop"),
        *search_nodes,
        Node("A", "Structure architect"),
        Node("OA", "Outline approval", "stop"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("I", "S"),
        *search_edges,
        Edge("A", "OA"),
        Edge("OA", "A", "restructuring, no cap", "back"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_abstract_only_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: you confirm", "stop"),
        Node("AB", "Abstract agent: a patch"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, (Edge("I", "AB"),))


def paper_citation_check_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("C", "Citation compliance: each claim against its source's full text"),
        Node(
            "SS",
            "The citation style to confirm, when more than half the entries fail the same check",
            "stop",
        ),
        Node(
            "SD",
            "A claim the run cannot support, or a reference that cannot be confirmed",
            "stop",
        ),
        Node("X", "Citation report"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("C", "SS"),
        Edge("SS", "C", "a fresh start, once", "back"),
        Edge("C", "SD", "a claim not in its source, or a reference no lookup confirms"),
        Edge("C", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_disclosure_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node(
            "S",
            "You name the venue, and paste the policy of a venue it does not know",
            "stop",
        ),
        Node("F", "Formatter: each kind of AI use marked, the statement as a patch"),
        Node("U", "You confirm each uncertain kind of AI use", "stop"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("S", "F"),
        Edge("F", "U", "a kind marked uncertain"),
        Edge("U", "F", "your answer", "back"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_plan_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: 3 questions, you confirm", "stop"),
        Node("M", "Socratic mentor, chapter by chapter, given each answer"),
        Node("Q", "The mentor's question", "stop"),
        Node("S", "Structure architect: the outline"),
        Node("M2", "Socratic mentor and argument builder: the stress test"),
        Node("X", "Chapter plan and INSIGHT collection"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("I", "M"),
        Edge("M", "Q"),
        Edge("Q", "M", "offers after 8 rounds on a chapter, 30 in all", "back"),
        Edge("M", "S"),
        Edge("S", "M2"),
        Edge("M2", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_format_convert_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("F", "Formatter: LaTeX, DOCX, PDF, Markdown, citation style, as patches"),
        Node(
            "F2",
            "Formatter, resumed: the conversion patch, when the citation style changes",
            "maybe",
        ),
        Node("CF", "Script: patches applied, compile, pandoc, the counts", "script"),
        Node("X", "Formatted document"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("F", "F2"),
        Edge("F", "CF"),
        Edge("F2", "CF"),
        Edge("CF", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_revision_coach_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("R", "Revision coach: the comments parsed"),
        Node("Q", "You confirm the parsed comments", "stop"),
        Node("R2", "Revision coach, resumed: roadmap, tracking template, letter skeleton"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("R", "Q"),
        Edge("Q", "R", "a correction", "back"),
        Edge("Q", "R2"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_rebuttal_audit_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("R", "Revision coach, parsing only: the comments and your response draft"),
        Node("X", "Coverage per comment, gaps, risk flags; advisory, nothing verified"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, (Edge("R", "X"),))


def paper_response_letter_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("W", "Draft writer: response-letter.md beside the manuscript"),
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer, fresh: a verdict per point"),
        Node("X", "The letter and its check"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, (Edge("W", "P"), Edge("P", "X")))


PAPER_MODES = (
    ("full", paper_full_graph),
    ("revision", paper_revision_graph),
    ("lit-review", paper_lit_review_graph),
    ("outline-only", paper_outline_only_graph),
    ("abstract-only", paper_abstract_only_graph),
    ("citation-check", paper_citation_check_graph),
    ("disclosure", paper_disclosure_graph),
    ("plan", paper_plan_graph),
    ("format-convert", paper_format_convert_graph),
    ("revision-coach", paper_revision_coach_graph),
    ("rebuttal-audit", paper_rebuttal_audit_graph),
    ("response-letter", paper_response_letter_graph),
)
EOF
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/docs/figures && python3 - <<'EOF'
S='/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/fig/simple.py'
new=open(S).read()
t=open('gen_figures.py').read()
a=t.index('def paper_full_graph()')
lr0=t.index('def paper_lit_review_graph()'); lr1=t.index('def paper_outline_only_graph()')
lit=t[lr0:lr1]
b=t.index('def paper_figures()')
# insert lit-review graph before outline-only in new
new=new.replace('def paper_outline_only_graph()', lit+'def paper_outline_only_graph()')
t=t[:a]+new+'\n\n'+t[b:]
open('gen_figures.py','w').write(t)
EOF
python3 gen_figures.py 2>&1 | tail -5
````

114. Bash, "Write the new detailed graph code to the scratchpad"

````
cat > /private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/fig/detailed.py <<'EOF'
GOAL = "[5.Q (i)]"
LETTER = "[5.Q item 5]"
SOURCES = "[5.Q item 6]"
PATCH_STOPS = (
    N(
        "PS",
        f"At every patch: a structural patch, a patch that does not apply, a script failed {GOAL}",
        "stop",
    ),
)
APPLY = f"Script, run by the session: anchorize, then apply each patch by its hashes {GOAL}"
CHECKS = (
    f"Script, run by the session: lookup.py cite-keys and check-urls; latexmk compile "
    f"with the engine of the class {GOAL}"
)
FIXES = f"Draft writer: the reference fix and the compile fix, when a check fails {GOAL}"
CLAIMS = (
    f"Citation compliance: format, DOI, retractions, and each claim against the passage "
    f"read in its source's full text {GOAL}"
)
CLAIM_STOP = (
    f"A claim the run cannot support, or a reference that cannot be confirmed, after every "
    f"claim is judged {GOAL}"
)
INCREMENTAL = f"Citation compliance, fresh: the cited sentences of the patch {GOAL}"
CLOSING = f"/writing review, unless you said no at intake {GOAL}"
FINDINGS = f"Draft writer: the findings you pick, or the autonomous pass, as a patch {GOAL}"


def paper_full_detailed() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N(
            "I0",
            f"Phase 0 Intake agent: config.md; citation verification, the number of sources "
            f"{SOURCES}, the closing review {GOAL}",
            "stop",
        ),
        N("Q1", "you skip Phase 1, giving your own sources?", "choice"),

115. Bash, "Splice the new detailed graphs into the comparison script"

````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/docs/figures/academic-paper-comparison && python3 - <<'EOF'
import re
D='/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/fig/detailed.py'
new=open(D).read()
t=open('gen_comparison.py').read()
def cut(name, kind='def '):
    global t
    a=t.index(kind+name)
    # find start of next top-level def/class/constant
    m=re.compile(r'\n(?=(def |class |[A-Z_]+ = |# ---))').search(t,a+5)
    b=m.start()+1
    seg=t[a:b]; t=t[:a]+t[b:]; return seg
for n in ['paper_full_detailed','paper_revision_detailed','paper_outline_only_detailed','paper_abstract_only_detailed','paper_citation_check_detailed','paper_disclosure_detailed','paper_plan_detailed','paper_format_convert_detailed','only_modes','only_modes_figure']:
    cut(n)
cut('Pair','class ')
cut('ONLY_NOTE','')
# lit-review intake node
o='''            "Intake agent: the draft and its questions, then config.md, DOI checking, the "
            "research question the run needs [D24, O, AD11, AV15]; a research report and its "
            "bibliography [AV1]; the profile [AV2]; a file in words [AQ]",'''
n='''            "Intake agent: config.md, citation verification, the number of sources "
            "[5.Q item 6], the research question the run needs [AD11, AV15]",'''
assert t.count(o)==1; t=t.replace(o,n)
a=t.index('# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# The figures.')
t=t[:a]+new+'\n\n'+t[a:]
open('gen_comparison.py','w').write(t)
EOF
grep -n "^def \|^class \|^[A-Z_]* = \|^# ---" gen_comparison.py

        "academic-paper: the formatter converts and compiles itself, with a hard gate on "
        "provenance markers. paper: the formatter writes patches and a report, and the "
        "session converts and counts. " + NOTE,
    ),
    (
        "revision-coach",
        "academic-paper 3.2.0",
        ap_revision_coach_detailed,
        paper_revision_coach_detailed,
        NOTE,
    ),
    (
        "rebuttal-audit",
        "academic-paper 3.2.0",
        ap_rebuttal_audit_detailed,
        paper_rebuttal_audit_detailed,
        NOTE,
    ),
    (
        "response-letter",
        "academic-pipeline external review protocol",
        ap_response_letter_detailed,
        paper_response_letter_detailed,
        "academic-paper has no such mode. " + NOTE,
    ),
)


'''
t=t[:a]+newtables+t[b:]
a=t.index('def figures()'); b=t.index('def main()')
fig='''def figures() -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
    """Every figure of this script, as the file name and its body."""
    paper_simple = dict(gf.PAPER_MODES)
    out: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for mode, caption, ap_build, note in SIMPLE:
        panels = [
            gf.Panel(caption, ap_build()),
            gf.Panel("paper", paper_simple[mode]()),
        ]
        out.append(
            (
                f"{mode}-simple.svg",
                gf.compose(
                    f"{mode}-simple.svg",
                    gf.describe(f"The {mode} mode, simple view", panels),
                    panels,
                    gf.SIMPLE_LEGEND,
                    note,
                ),
            )
        )
    for mode, caption, ap_build, paper_build, note in DETAILED:
        panels = [
            gf.Panel(caption, ap_build()),
            gf.Panel("paper, as built", paper_build()),
        ]
        out.append(
            (
                f"{mode}-detailed.svg",
                gf.compose(
                    f"{mode}-detailed.svg",
                    gf.describe(f"The {mode} mode, detailed view", panels),
                    panels,
                    detailed_legend(panels),
                    note,
                ),
            )
        )
    out.append(("academic-pipeline.svg", pipeline_figure()))
    return tuple(out)


'''
t=t[:a]+fig+t[b:]
open('gen_comparison.py','w').write(t)
EOF
python3 gen_comparison.py 2>&1 | tail -4

import re,html
D='docs/figures/academic-paper-comparison/'
def alt(f):
    s=open(D+f).read()
    m=re.search(r'<title>(.*?)</title>',s,re.S); t=html.unescape(m.group(1))
    t=t.replace('[','(').replace(']',')').replace(' (agent)','')
    t=t.replace('. Boxes: ',': ',1)
    return t
modes=['full','revision','lit-review','outline-only','abstract-only','citation-check','disclosure','plan','format-convert','revision-coach','rebuttal-audit','response-letter']
lead="Each mode of `paper` beside the same mode of academic-paper 3.2.0, drawn by `docs/figures/academic-paper-comparison/gen_comparison.py`. The response-letter mode, which academic-paper does not have, is drawn beside steps 3 and 4 of academic-pipeline's external review protocol, which it follows. The simple view draws the agent starts, the stops and the cycles, and the detailed view draws every step, script and stop. One rule holds in every mode and is not drawn: at every patch, a structural patch, a patch that does not apply or a failed script stops the run. Since `paper` has every mode of academic-paper, no mode is left that only academic-paper has."
out=["## paper against academic-paper","",lead,""]
for m in modes:
    out+=[f"### {m}","",f"![{alt(m+'-simple.svg')}](figures/academic-paper-comparison/{m}-simple.svg)","",f"![{alt(m+'-detailed.svg')}](figures/academic-paper-comparison/{m}-detailed.svg)",""]
p='docs/academic-coverage.md'; t=open(p).read()
a=t.index("## paper against academic-paper"); b=t.index("### academic-pipeline and where its stages go")
t=t[:a]+"\n".join(out)+"\n"+t[b:]
open(p,'w').write(t)

t=open(P).read()
a=t.index("\n## academic-paper\n"); b=t.index("\n## academic-paper-reviewer\n")
sec=t[a:b].split("\n")
out=[]
for line in sec:
    if line.startswith("| `"):
        f=line.split("`")[1]
        if f in AP and AP[f] is not None:
            mark,reason=AP[f]; line=f"| `{f}` | {mark} | {reason} |"
            AP[f]=None
    out.append(line)
left=[k for k,v in AP.items() if v is not None]
assert not left,left

````

def rep(prefix,new):

    assert len(idx)==1,prefix
    if new is None: del L[idx[0]]

rep("- **chapter plan**:","- **chapter plan**: the file `chapter-plan.md` that a plan-mode run of `paper` ends with: for each chapter its core argument, its supporting evidence and its expected word count, written by the argument builder after the stress test. It is not an Ordo plan, which is a ledger folder `/plan` opens for a roadmap entry. Stated in: `skills/paper/SKILL.md`, Steps, \"plan\" item 5; `skills/paper/references/plan-mode-protocol.md`.")
rep("- **draft review**:","- **draft review**: the review of the author's own draft by the peer reviewer. It runs inside `paper`'s full mode as Phase 6 of the Generator-Evaluator Contract, and as the call `/paper draft-review <main.tex>` on an existing manuscript, where it writes one review and changes nothing. In full mode a revision decision sends the draft to the draft writer for one rewrite, as one patch over every block, and a second round. After round 2 a score of 6.5 or more, or below 6.5 with every Critical issue resolved, goes on, and the issues left become acknowledged limitations. A score below 6.5 with a Critical issue unresolved stops the run at \"The draft review does not pass\". It is neither a panel review nor a referee review. Stated in: `skills/paper/SKILL.md`, \"full\" item 10 and \"draft-review\"; `skills/paper/references/peer-reviewer.md`, \"Revision Progress Tracking (Max 2 Rounds)\".")
rep("- **intake agent**:","- **intake agent**: the fresh agent that holds intake (Phase 0) in a `paper` run of full, outline-only, abstract-only, lit-review or plan mode. It runs the configuration interview, writes `config.md`, and the session shows the record for the user's confirmation. The other modes have no intake agent, and the session asks what they need. Stated in: `skills/paper/SKILL.md`, Steps 6; `skills/paper/references/intake.md`.")
rep("- **part, of a research question**:",None)
rep("- **plan mode**:","- **plan mode**: the mode `/paper plan`, in which a Socratic mentor questions the user chapter by chapter, the structure architect writes the outline and the argument builder writes the chapter plan after the stress test, so that the user decides what the paper argues before any text is drafted. It is neither the `plan` skill, which opens a plan for a roadmap entry, nor Claude Code's own plan mode. Stated in: `skills/paper/SKILL.md`, Steps, \"plan\"; `skills/paper/references/plan-mode-protocol.md`.")
rep("- **reading of the findings pass**:",None)
open(P,'w').write('\n'.join(L))
EOF

````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q && python3 - <<'EOF'
import re,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'docs/figures')
import gen_figures as gf
K={"agent":"","maybe":", dashed","stop":", a stop","script":", a grey box for a script the session runs","choice":", a rounded box where the session judges"}
modes=[m for m,_ in gf.PAPER_MODES]
lines=[]
for m,b in gf.PAPER_MODES:
    g=b()
    boxes="; ".join(n.label+K[n.kind] for n in (*g.nodes,*g.floating))
    lines.append(f"![The {m} mode of paper: {boxes}.](docs/figures/paper/{m}.svg)")
P='README.md'; t=open(P).read().split('\n')
s=[i for i,l in enumerate(t) if l.startswith("Each mode of `paper` has its figure.")][0]
e=[i for i,l in enumerate(t) if l.startswith("![The format-convert mode of paper")][0]

for l in lines: new+= [l,""]
new=new[:-1]
t=t[:s]+new+t[e+1:]
row=[i for i,l in enumerate(t) if l.startswith("| `paper` |")][0]
t[row]="| `paper` | Writes, revises, plans and formats a LaTeX manuscript in academic-paper's twelve modes (full, outline-only, revision, abstract-only, lit-review, format-convert, citation-check, plan, revision-coach, disclosure, rebuttal-audit and response-letter) and two calls, `/paper draft-review` and `/paper figures`. Its twelve roles are academic-paper's, each a fresh agent: intake, literature strategist, structure architect, argument builder, draft writer, citation compliance, abstract, peer reviewer, formatter, Socratic mentor, visualization and revision coach, and each role's page is academic-paper's page copied. A plain request picks the mode. Full mode runs academic-paper's eight phases, with the outline approval, the draft writer and the peer reviewer as the Generator-Evaluator Contract's four calls, and at most two revision rounds. Plan mode guides you chapter by chapter with the Socratic mentor to a chapter plan. Revision-coach parses reviewer comments into a revision roadmap, rebuttal-audit checks a response you wrote, and revision and response-letter write the response letter and check it against the revised manuscript. Format-convert converts a manuscript to DOCX, PDF, Markdown or LaTeX, to another citation style or to a venue's template, with `pandoc`, `latexmk`, `pdftocairo`, `pdftotext` and, for a `.bib`, `latex` and `bibtex` when they are installed. Every change to the manuscript is a patch applied by script. Full, revision and citation-check runs judge each cited claim against a passage read from the source in the run, and check the cited sentences of each later patch. A lit-review run writes an annotated bibliography, a literature matrix and a synthesis analysis, and the session resolves the DOI or arXiv ID of every entry of its `.bib` with `lookup.py resolve`. Intake takes an optional number of sources, which the session splits over the strategist starts. The run's records stay in `paper-runs/` beside the manuscript's folder. Full and revision runs end with `/writing`, and you reply with the findings to apply as patches or ask for an autonomous writer pass |"
open(P,'w').write('\n'.join(t))
EOF

````

t[row]="| `paper` | Writes, revises, plans and formats a LaTeX manuscript in academic-paper's twelve modes (full, outline-only, revision, abstract-only, lit-review, format-convert, citation-check, plan, revision-coach, disclosure, rebuttal-audit and response-letter) and two calls, `/paper draft-review` and `/paper figures`. Its twelve roles are academic-paper's, each a fresh agent: intake, literature strategist, structure architect, argument builder, draft writer, citation compliance, abstract, peer reviewer, formatter, Socratic mentor, visualization and revision coach, and each role's page is academic-paper's page copied. A plain request picks the mode. Full mode runs academic-paper's eight phases, with the outline approval, the draft writer and the peer reviewer as the Generator-Evaluator Contract's four calls, and at most two revision rounds. Plan mode guides you chapter by chapter with the Socratic mentor to a chapter plan. Revision-coach parses reviewer comments into a revision roadmap, rebuttal-audit checks a response you wrote, and revision and response-letter write the response letter and check it against the revised manuscript. Format-convert converts a manuscript to DOCX, PDF, Markdown or LaTeX, to another citation style or to a venue's template, with `pandoc`, `latexmk`, `pdftocairo`, `pdftotext` and, for a `.bib`, `latex` and `bibtex` when they are installed. Every change to the manuscript is a patch applied by script. Full, revision and citation-check runs judge each cited claim against a passage read from the source in the run, and check the cited sentences of each later patch. A lit-review run writes an annotated bibliography, a literature matrix and a synthesis analysis, and the session resolves the DOI or arXiv ID of every entry of its `.bib` with `lookup.py resolve`. Intake takes an optional number of sources, which the session splits over the strategist starts. The run's records stay in `paper-runs/` beside the manuscript's folder. Full and revision runs end with `/writing`, and you reply with the findings to apply as patches or ask for an autonomous writer pass |"
open(P,'w').write('\n'.join(t))
EOF
sed -n 76,102p README.md | cut -c1-200
````

### The scratchpad files those commands run

The commands above run these files by path (`python3 -` with the file as input, or `from ed import ed`). Each is quoted whole.

`ed.py`:

````
import sys
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'
def ed(path, pairs):
    p = R + path
    t = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
    for a, b in pairs:
        n = t.count(a)
        if n != 1:
            raise SystemExit(f"{path}: {a[:80]!r} occurs {n} times")
        t = t.replace(a, b)
    open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(t)
    if open(p, encoding='utf-8').read() != t:
        raise SystemExit(f"{path}: read back differs")
    print(f"{path}: {len(pairs)} edits")
````

`charmap-cmd.txt`:

````
python3 - $(cat pages.txt) <<'PY'
import re, sys
BOX = {'<U+2500>':'-','<U+2501>':'-','<U+2550>':'=','<U+2502>':'|','<U+251C>':'+','<U+2514>':'+','<U+250C>':'+','<U+2510>':'+','<U+2518>':'+','<U+2524>':'+','<U+252C>':'+','<U+2534>':'+','<U+253C>':'+'}
SIMPLE = {'<U+2260>':'!=','<U+2265>':'>=','<U+2264>':'<=','<U+00D7>':'x','<U+2208>':'in','<U+00B1>':'+/-','<U+2229>':'&','<U+21D2>':'=>','<U+25A1>':'[ ]','<U+2610>':'[ ]','<U+2713>':'[x]','<U+2026>':'...','<U+03B7>':'eta','<U+00B9>':'^1','<U+00B2>':'^2','<U+2074>':'^4','<U+2075>':'^5','<U+00B6>':'paragraph'}
def dash(line):
    line = re.sub(r'\|\s*<U+2014>\s*(?=\|)', '| - ', line)
    line = re.sub(r'(\d)<U+2013>(\d)', r'\1-\2', line)
    line = line.replace(' <U+2013> ', ' <U+2014> ').replace('<U+2013>', '-')
    if re.match(r'\s*(#+ |[-*] |\d+\. |\*\*)', line) and ' <U+2014> ' in line:
        head, tail = line.split(' <U+2014> ', 1)
        if len(head.split()) <= 12:
            line = head + ': ' + tail
    line = re.sub(r'\s*<U+2014>\s*', ', ', line)
    line = line.replace(', ,', ',').replace(',,', ',').replace(':,', ':').replace('(, ', '(')
    return line
for path in sys.argv[1:]:
    text = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    out, code = [], False
    for line in text.split('\n'):
        if line.lstrip().startswith('```'):
            code = not code
        for k, v in BOX.items():
            line = line.replace(k, v)
        line = re.sub('[<U+26A0><U+2605>]<U+FE0F>? ?', '', line).replace(' NEW', ' NEW') if ('<U+26A0>' in line or '<U+2605>' in line) else line
        line = line.replace('<U+00A7><U+00A7>', 'sections ').replace('<U+00A7> ', 'section ').replace('<U+00A7>', 'section ')
        for k, v in SIMPLE.items():
            line = line.replace(k, v)
        if code:
            line = line.replace('<U+2192>', '->').replace('<U+2190>', '<-').replace('<U+2193>', 'v').replace('<U+2194>', '<->')
        else:
            line = re.sub(r'\s*<U+2192>\s*', ' to ', line)
        line = dash(line)
        out.append(line)
    new = '\n'.join(out)
    open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(new)
    if open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() != new:
        sys.exit(path + ': read back differs')
PY
````

`rolemap-cmd.txt`:

````
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
SP = '/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/'
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'
ROLE = {'intake':'intake agent','literature_strategist':'literature strategist','structure_architect':'structure architect','argument_builder':'argument builder','draft_writer':'draft writer','citation_compliance':'citation compliance agent','abstract_bilingual':'abstract agent','peer_reviewer':'peer reviewer','formatter':'formatter','socratic_mentor':'Socratic mentor','visualization':'visualization agent','revision_coach':'revision coach','format':'formatter'}
alt = '|'.join(sorted(ROLE, key=len, reverse=True))
rows = [l.rstrip('\n').split('\t') for l in open(SP + 'map.tsv')]
def cap(s): return s[0].upper() + s[1:]
for s, p in rows:
    path = R + p
    t = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    o = t
    t = re.sub(r'^name: (' + alt + r')_agent$', lambda m: 'name: ' + p.split('/')[-1][:-3], t, flags=re.M)
    t = re.sub(r'\| `(' + alt + r')_agent`', lambda m: '| ' + cap(ROLE[m.group(1)]), t)
    t = re.sub(r'\[(' + alt + r')_agent\]', lambda m: '[' + ROLE[m.group(1)] + ']', t)
    t = re.sub(r'\[(' + alt + r')_agent \+ (' + alt + r')_agent\]', lambda m: '[' + ROLE[m.group(1)] + ' + ' + ROLE[m.group(2)] + ']', t)
    t = re.sub(r'(?<=^  - )(' + alt + r')_agent$', lambda m: ROLE[m.group(1)], t, flags=re.M)
    def prose(m):
        pre, role = m.group(1), ROLE[m.group(2)]
        if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $', pre):
            return pre + role
        start = pre == '' or re.search(r'(^|[.!?]\s|^\s*(?:[-*]|\d+\.)\s|\*\*|^#+ |\(|: )$', pre)
        return pre + ('The ' if start and not pre.endswith('(') and not pre.endswith(': ') else 'the ') + role
    t = re.sub(r'(^[^\n]*?|)`?\b(' + alt + r')_agent\b`?', lambda m: prose(m), t, flags=re.M) if False else t
    out = []
    for line in t.split('\n'):
        while True:
            m = re.search(r'`?\b(' + alt + r')_agent\b`?', line)
            if not m: break
            pre = line[:m.start()]
            role = ROLE[m.group(1)]
            if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $', pre):
                rep = role
            elif re.search(r'(^\s*$|[.!?]\s+$|^\s*(?:[-*>]|\d+\.)\s+(\*\*)?$|^\s*\*\*$|^#+ $)', pre):
                rep = 'The ' + role
            else:
                rep = 'the ' + role
            line = pre + rep + line[m.end():]
        out.append(line)
    t = '\n'.join(out)
    if t != o:
        open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(t)
        assert open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() == t
        print(p)
PY
````

`refmap-cmd.txt`:

````
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
SP = '/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/'
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'
rows = [l.rstrip('\n').split('\t') for l in open(SP + 'map.tsv')]
names = {s.split('/')[-1]: p for s, p in rows}
names['literature_strategist_agent.md'] = 'references/literature-strategist.md'
alt = '|'.join(re.escape(n) for n in sorted(names, key=len, reverse=True))
pat = re.compile(r'(?:\.\./)*(?:academic-paper/|academic-paper-reviewer/|academic-pipeline/)?(?:(?:references|agents|templates|examples)/)?(' + alt + r')(?![A-Za-z0-9_])')
total = 0
for s, p in rows:
    path = R + p
    t = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    new, n = pat.subn(lambda m: names[m.group(1)], t)
    if n:
        open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(new)
        assert open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() == new
        print(p, n)
        total += n
print('total', total)

python3 - references/funding-statement-guide.md references/funding-statement-template.md references/credit-statement-template.md references/credit-authorship-guide.md references/literature-review-example.md <<'PY'
import re,sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    t=open(p).read(); o=t
    t=re.sub(r'\n\*\*(?:[A-Za-z ]+ )?\(?Chinese(?: Format Example| Version| Template)?\)?\*\*:?\n+```[a-z]*\n.*?\n```\n', '\n', t, flags=re.S)
    t=re.sub(r'\n\*\*No Conflict of Interest\*\* \(Chinese\):\n```\n.*?\n```\n', '\n', t, flags=re.S)
    t=re.sub(r'^\|[^\n]*Chinese format[^\n]*\|\n', '', t, flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r'^- \[ \] English papers use English statements; Chinese papers use Chinese statements\n', '', t, flags=re.M)
    t=t.replace('3. **Both Chinese and English declarations required**: If the paper is in English, use English for the funding statement; if in Chinese, use Chinese\n4. **Open Access fees**', '3. **Open Access fees**')
    if '## Chinese Abstract' in t:
        a=t.index('## Chinese Abstract'); b=t.index('## 1. Introduction')
        t=t[:a]+t[b:]
    t=re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', t)
    if t!=o: open(p,'w').write(t); print(p)

    for b in L:
        if b.lstrip().startswith(('```','~~~')):
            f=not f; out.append(b); continue
        if not f and out:
            a=out[-1]
            ok = a.strip() and b.strip() and len(a)>=70 and not a.lstrip().startswith(('|','#','>','<!--','---','```')) \
                 and not b.lstrip().startswith(('|','#','>','```','<!--')) and not re.match(r'\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)]|- \[[ x]\]) ',b) \
                 and not a.rstrip().endswith(('  ','\\'))
            if ok:
                out[-1]=a.rstrip()+' '+b.strip(); n+=1; continue
        out.append(b)
    if n: open(p,'w').write('\n'.join(out)); print(p,n)
PY
````

import re,sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    t=open(p).read(); o=t
    t=re.sub(r'^\*\*Status\*\*:.*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=t.replace('**Parent skill**: `academic-paper`','**Parent skill**: `paper`')
    t=t.replace("ARS's v3.2 `disclosure` mode","`paper`'s `disclosure` mode").replace('ARS does not','`paper` does not').replace('What ARS did','What `paper` did')
    t=re.sub(r' ?\((?:same as )?v3\.2(?:, default)?\)', lambda m: ' (default)' if 'default' in m.group(0) else '', t)
    t=re.sub(r' ?\(#108(?: path| extension[^)]*)?\)','',t)
    t=re.sub(r' ?\((?:v3\.2 )?path, default\)',' (default)',t)
    t=t.replace("v3.2's venue track",'the venue track').replace('v3.2 venue path','the venue path').replace('v3.2 venue track','the venue track').replace('v3.2 path','venue path')
    t=re.sub(r'\bv3\.2 ','',t)
    t=re.sub(r'\b#108 ','',t)
    t=re.sub(r' ?\(#108 \+ v3\.2\)','',t)
    t=t.replace('`shared/policy_data/nature_policy.md`','the Nature policy text of `references/venue-disclosure-policies.md`')
    if t!=o: open(p,'w').write(t); print(p)
PY
python3 - references/policy-anchor-disclosure-protocol.md references/policy-anchor-table.md <<'PY'
import re,sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    t=open(p).read(); o=t
    t=re.sub(r'^.*is mirrored by the conformance referee.*$', lambda m: m.group(0).split(' The recognition logic above is mirrored')[0], t, flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r'^- Shared Nature policy source.*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r' ?\(concern #\d+(?: resolution)?\)','',t)
    t=re.sub(r'\(concern #\d+ resolution; ','(',t)
    t=re.sub(r' \(concern #\d+ resolution\)','',t)
    t=t.replace('applying the **concern #6 resolution**','applying the **per-facet rule**').replace("behind concern #6's resolution","behind the per-facet rule").replace('per concern #6 resolution','per the per-facet rule').replace('concern #5 resolution = ','').replace('**Concern #10 resolution (','**Substantive-content gate (')
    t=t.replace('Per section 4.3 G7 invariant + section 4.5 / section 4.6 cell #15,','Per the G7 invariant,').replace('(section 4.3 G3 / G10 invariant)','(G3 / G10 invariant)').replace('The section 4.3 invariants constrain','The invariants constrain').replace('This table is the section 4.3 ','This table is the ').replace('The section 4.3 G7 invariant','The G7 invariant')
    t=t.replace('**Forbidden by Decision Doc section 4.3 (renderer must honour these):**','**Forbidden (renderer must honour these):**').replace('(Decision Doc section 4.3 G8 invariant)','(G8 invariant)').replace('via the section 4.4 #5 hybrid output channel','via the hybrid output channel')
    t=re.sub(r'^\*\*Provenance\*\*:.*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r'^\*\*Lint contract\*\*:.*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r'^- (?:Decision Doc \(parent\)|Implementation spec \(parent\)|Discovery doc).*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=t.replace('the renderer routes to this anchor only when the upstream pipeline (or cold-start `mode=` input) sets `slr_lineage=true`.','the renderer routes to this anchor only when the `mode=systematic-review` input selects it.')
    if t!=o: open(p,'w').write(t); print(p)
PY
````

import re
S='/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/SKILL.md'
old=open('old-format-convert.sec').read()
lead="Text formats: LaTeX (.tex + .bib), DOCX (via Pandoc), PDF (via LaTeX or Pandoc), Markdown. Citation formats: APA 7.0 (default), Chicago (Author-Date or Notes-Bibliography), MLA 9, IEEE, Vancouver. The formatter supports late-stage citation format conversion between any two supported formats via \"Convert citations to [format]\".\n\n"
E=[
("an unfinished run, the intake confirmation, a structural","a structural"),
("Intake (Steps 6) asks the rows `references/intake.md` marks for format-convert.","Format-convert has no intake agent. The session asks the user, in one message, for what the items below name and the call does not give, and writes the answers into `config.md` (Steps 6): the targets, the Format Profile of `references/intake.md`, Step 5, and the Existing Materials, such as a reference `.docx`, a CSL file or a venue's template."),
("1. Settle the targets at intake.","1. Settle the targets from the call and the session's question (Steps 6)."),
("A request with no target takes Markdown, and the intake confirmation shows it.","A call with no target takes Markdown, and the session's question names it."),
("4. Start the formatter (Steps 8 and 9) the first time.","4. Start the formatter (Steps 8 and 9)."),
("It reads these rows of `config.md`: Target formats, Citation style, Venue, Venue limits, Cover letter, Format profile, Materials in hand, Paper type and the Notes.","It reads these rows of `config.md`: Output Format, Citation Format, Target Journal, Format Profile, Existing Materials and Paper Type."),
("When it writes a cover letter it also reads the rows Authors, Human or patient data and AI use. In lit-review mode it reads the row Authors (`references/formatter.md`, \"What the agent reads\").","When it writes a cover letter it also reads the row Co-Authors (`references/formatter.md`, \"Cover Letter Generation\")."),
("Item 6 starts the formatter again for them, after the compile of the new style.","Item 6 resumes the formatter for them, after the compile of the new style."),
("compile the copy and start the formatter again for the conversion patch.","compile the copy and resume the formatter for the conversion patch."),
("Start the formatter (Steps 8 and 9) a second time, as a fresh agent. It is given `config.md`, the files of `convert/` with their manifests and patch paths, the compile's `.aux` and `.bbl`, the first start's `format-report.md` and the path of `convert/`.","Resume the formatter (Steps 8). It is given the files of `convert/` with their manifests and patch paths, the compile's `.aux` and `.bbl` and the path of `convert/`."),
("The intake confirmation names each such file","The session's question (Steps 6) names each such file"),
("is replaced only after the intake confirmation has named it.","is replaced only after the session's question (Steps 6) has named it and the user has confirmed it."),
("   - It lists each field a limit of the Venue limits row made the venue's value (field, the limit, the value overridden).\n",""),
]
for o,n in E:
    c=old.count(o)
    assert c>=1,(o,c)
    old=old.replace(o,n)
old=old.replace("Materials in hand","Existing Materials").replace("Format profile row","Format Profile row")
txt=open(S).read()
a=txt.index("### format-convert\n")+len("### format-convert\n")
b=txt.index("### citation-check\n")
txt=txt[:a]+"\n"+lead+old.lstrip("\n")+("" if old.endswith("\n\n") else "\n")+txt[b:]
open(S,'w').write(txt)

"SKILL.md":("rebuild: paper","The file sets twelve agents, eleven modes, the eight phases with their checkpoint rules, the Generator-Evaluator Contract of full mode, the revision patch protocol, the rebuttal audit, the failure paths, the anti-patterns and the quality standards. The zh-TW triggers and the bilingual abstract, the RAISE compliance agent, Mode A of `academic-pipeline`, the version notes and the integration list are left out. The research stage and the integrity check of a finished manuscript go to `researcher`, the external review to `paper-review`."),
"agents/abstract_bilingual_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","Language-neutral rules ask for five parts, 150 to 300 words, five to seven keywords outside the title, no citations and defined abbreviations. The abstract agent writes the abstract and the keywords as one patch in full and abstract-only mode, held in `skills/paper/SKILL.md`."),
"agents/argument_builder_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","A thesis rests on three to five claim-evidence-reasoning chains with counter-arguments and rebuttals, and two weak-argument signs return the argument for strengthening. "agents/citation_compliance_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","Citations and references are checked both ways and for DOI form, self-citation, age and retractions, and a claim not in its source stops the check. Chinese citation rules stay out."),
"agents/draft_writer_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","The writer drafts sections in topic-evidence-explanation-link paragraphs, cites sources predating the events they evidence, and revises by hashed block patches.
